        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: VibeComfy/Wan2GP Parity Closure

## Overview
The current coordinated workspace already has most of the parity scaffolding: VibeComfy has pure-Python ready templates, template-index refresh/check tooling, `validate`, `doctor`, and model-asset extraction; `reigh-worker` has route selectors, capability contracts, app inventory, live matrix metadata, and tracker rendering; `reigh-worker-orchestrator` is route-contract aware; `runpod-lifecycle` has schema-safe pod status/liveness primitives.

Prep executed in this planning pass: no file edits. Cheap checks run:
- `vibecomfy`: `python -m vibecomfy.cli validate video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame` -> ok
- `vibecomfy`: `python -m tools.refresh_template_index --check && python -m pytest tests/test_ready_templates.py -q` -> 39 passed
- `reigh-worker`: `python -m pytest tests/test_template_routing.py -q` -> 92 passed
- `reigh-worker`: `python -m pytest tests/test_capability_contracts.py -q` -> 12 passed
- `reigh-worker-orchestrator`: route-aware/stale-worker tests -> 6 passed
- `reigh-worker`: `python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report validate --json` currently fails with 4 route-binding errors for the two LTX first/last no-control routes.

The concrete root blocker has changed from the brief: `source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py` now marks `travel_segment__model-ltx2__guidance-none__continuity-first_last__profile-default` and `travel_segment__model-ltx2_distilled__guidance-none__continuity-first_last__profile-default` as `vibecomfy_supported` with template `video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame`, but `scripts/capability_contracts/contracts.json` and `docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md` still classify those same route keys as unsupported/fail-closed. That mismatch is the first thing to fix.

## Phase 1: Contract Truth And Drift Gates

### Step 1: Align LTX first/last no-control contracts (`reigh-worker/scripts/capability_contracts/contracts.json`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Promote the two LTX no-control first/last route contracts from `implementation: unsupported` / `status: unsupported_fail_closed` to `implementation: vibecomfy` with `template_id: video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame`.
2. Preserve the current fail-closed state for the LTX control-guide routes: `ltx_control_video`, `ltx_control_pose`, `ltx_control_depth`, `ltx_control_canny`, and `ltx_control_cameraman` remain unsupported until control-capable template proof exists.
3. Replace stale blockers with static evidence from VibeComfy validation and template index presence. Keep status at `static_valid` or `template_bound`, not `runtime_green`, until live evidence exists.
4. Update artifact contract fields to match travel segment behavior: MP4 output, child-task DB completion semantics, and storage/output path expectations.

### Step 2: Render tracker from contracts (`reigh-worker/docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Regenerate the tracker using `python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report render docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md`.
2. Confirm the two no-control LTX first/last rows are no longer listed in the unsupported table, while the LTX control rows still show explicit fail-closed blockers.

### Step 3: Add an executable route-contract validation gate (`reigh-worker/tests/test_capability_contracts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Add a regression test that invokes `report.validate` or `validate_capability_evidence(...)` and asserts there are no route-binding errors.
2. This closes the current gap where direct loader validation reports the mismatch, but the existing pytest file still passes.

## Phase 2: Worker Adapter And Matrix Discoverability

### Step 4: Prove LTX first/last child routing in worker tests (`reigh-worker/tests/test_template_routing.py`, `reigh-worker/tests/test_vibecomfy_backend_selection.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Add route-selector tests for both LTX no-control route keys showing `should_use_vibecomfy is True` and the selected template is `video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame`.
2. Add a travel-child adapter test analogous to the existing Wan VACE test that uses an LTX model name, first/last image inputs, default profile, and no control guidance.
3. Assert the resolved params preserve `start_image`, `start_image_url`, `end_image`, `end_image_url`, prompt, seed, resolution, frames/FPS, and any negative prompt fields that Wan2GP currently preserves.

### Step 5: Add stable live matrix cases for current route names (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/matrix.py`, `reigh-worker/scripts/capability_contracts/live_matrix_manifest.json`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Add discoverable cases for the current LTX no-control first/last child routes, using names such as `travel_segment_ltx2_first_last` and `travel_segment_ltx2_distilled_first_last`.
2. Do not resurrect the stale `travel_segment_ltx2_control_video_first_last` name.
3. Add manifest entries only for stable case metadata; do not claim runtime evidence until a real report exists.
4. Keep filters by case name, task type, and route key working so future agents can find the exact LTX rows without grepping old names.

## Phase 3: VibeComfy Template Binding Check

### Step 6: Verify pure-Python template inputs (`vibecomfy/ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Inspect how this ready template binds first frame, last frame, prompt, seed, dimensions, frames, and FPS.
2. If runtime injection depends on `READY_METADATA["unbound_inputs"]`, add explicit bindings for the app-controlled fields instead of leaving them as baked source-workflow defaults.
3. Keep the template pure Python; do not introduce JSON execution.
4. Run `python -m vibecomfy.cli validate video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame` and `python -m vibecomfy.cli doctor video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame --json` after any template edits.

### Step 7: Strengthen ready-template linting (`vibecomfy/tests/test_ready_templates.py` or a focused new test)
**Scope:** Small
1. Add a lint that app-exposed ready templates used by worker contracts declare enough `unbound_inputs` or metadata for route-controlled values.
2. Start with LTX first/last and avoid a broad rule that breaks unrelated supplemental templates.

## Phase 4: Validation And Runtime Evidence

### Step 8: Run cheap static validation first
**Scope:** Small
1. In `vibecomfy`: run `python -m tools.refresh_template_index --check`, `python -m vibecomfy.cli validate video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame`, and targeted ready-template/model-asset tests.
2. In `reigh-worker`: run `python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report validate --json`, `python -m pytest tests/test_capability_contracts.py tests/test_template_routing.py tests/test_vibecomfy_backend_selection.py -q`.
3. In `reigh-worker-orchestrator`: rerun route-aware task count and stale-worker tests only if route metadata or contract versioning changes.

### Step 9: Promote only with live evidence
**Scope:** Medium
1. Run LTX live matrix cases only after static route/contract/template checks are green and there is no duplicate main-thread RunPod work in progress.
2. If live runs are launched, terminate pods, save report paths, and add the case IDs to `live_evidence` only for completed outputs with artifact locations.
3. Promote from `static_valid`/`template_bound` to `runtime_green` only after live report evidence exists.

## Execution Order
1. Fix the worker contract/tracker mismatch before touching runtime behavior.
2. Add the regression gate that makes `report validate` failures visible in pytest.
3. Prove adapter payload preservation for LTX no-control first/last routes.
4. Add matrix case discoverability after the route and contract names are stable.
5. Validate VibeComfy template input binding before any live RunPod attempt.
6. Run live evidence last and only if it will not duplicate main-thread work.

## Validation Order
1. `python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report validate --json`
2. `python -m pytest tests/test_capability_contracts.py -q`
3. `python -m pytest tests/test_template_routing.py tests/test_vibecomfy_backend_selection.py -q`
4. `cd ../vibecomfy && python -m tools.refresh_template_index --check`
5. `cd ../vibecomfy && python -m vibecomfy.cli validate video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame`
6. `cd ../vibecomfy && python -m pytest tests/test_ready_templates.py tests/test_model_assets.py -q`
7. Optional runtime: targeted LTX live matrix cases only after static gates pass.

## Remaining Blockers
- Worker contracts currently disagree with worker route source for the two no-control LTX first/last routes.
- LTX control routes still lack proof that the pinned first/last template is control-capable; they should remain fail-closed.
- Runtime evidence for LTX first/last VibeComfy travel child routes is not yet present in stable matrix reports.
- The VibeComfy LTX first/last template validates structurally, but app-field binding needs explicit review before claiming full settings parity.

  "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-108",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-145",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-153",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: worker integration: the migration doc describes the per-call profile override gap as involving reigh-worker adapter handling of override_profile before constructing vibecomfy sessionconfig, but the plan limits sprint 1 per-run work to the vibecomfy cli/runtime. that may be acceptable for a vibecomfy-library mvp, but it does not fully exercise the production-facing per-task override semantics called out in the overall context.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-155",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: a residual issue-hint tension remains for resolution handling. the plan acknowledges current `vibecomfy run` lacks a resolution flag, but allows the adapter to leave resolution and other template-specific values unwired for sprint 2. because `z_image_turbo` and `qwen_image_2512` are marked vibecomfy-supported direct routes, this may not preserve the existing worker task-parameter contract for tasks that provide a non-default `resolution`.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "direct-route-parameter-parity": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-160",

      "occurrence_count": 1,

        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    }

  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-142",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-154",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: documentation/data gap: the sprint exit criteria require profiles 1 and 3 to have vram and wall-clock data, but the plan puts that only in metadata as an info-level criterion and does not schedule a concrete artifact location or command for recording the measurements. the implementation could satisfy all unit tests while still missing one of the explicit exit criteria.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting infrastructure for resolution is still incomplete if direct vibecomfy routes are considered production-supported. vibecomfy has a `vibecomfy.patches.resolution` helper and the cli can load scratchpad files with `allow_scratchpad=true`, but the plan does not require using that path; it permits leaving resolution out for sprint 2.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-162",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: checked vibecomfy profile wording against `vibecomfy/tests/test_ready_templates.py`. the revised plan says to add the production template to representative compile profile coverage 'alongside the existing dry-run id', but the dry-run id is not currently in `profile_smoke_template_ids`; only `video/wanvideo_wrapper_22_5b_i2v` and `video/wan_t2v` are. this is a small wording mismatch rather than a functional blocker because adding either the production id alone or both ids would still satisfy sprint 4.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-4-wan-single-frame-and-20260506-0818"
      ]
    }

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-146",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: finding callers of the changed module shows that `shared.utils.self_refiner` is consumed by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx2_handler.py`, multiple ltx2 pipeline modules, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`. the revised validation steps still only drive the three bridge getters in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`; none of the scheduled commands execute a real `self_refiner` caller, so the plan does not yet verify the main caller shapes for the other explicit sprint 2 file change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-159",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked direct queue caller values in `db_task_to_generation_task()`: direct image tasks may carry `resolution`, `steps`/`num_inference_steps`, `seed`, and `override_profile`. the revised adapter plan covers seed, steps, and memory profile but not guaranteed resolution propagation, so it does not handle all relevant direct-route caller arguments for a supported vibecomfy direct route.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    }
  ],

        Escalated debt subsystems:
        [
  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 9,
    "plan_count": 6,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-143",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-156",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-164",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: route support states: the revised step 5 says to classify ltx control rows as vibecomfy_supported, wgp_only, vibecomfy_unsupported, new, or blocked, but the current worker routesupportstate enum at reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py:17-20 only supports wgp_only, vibecomfy_supported, and vibecomfy_unsupported. the plan needs to either extend the enum/report schema for new/blocked or keep new/blocked strictly as fixture disposition values mapped to an existing runtime support state.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-9-control-rail-and-20260506-1802"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-165",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: startup tests: step 11 still needs sharper wording for `vibecomfy_memory_profile`. the current startup template exports it only inside `if [ \"$reigh_backend\" = \"vibecomfy\" ] && [[ \"$reigh_worker_profile\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]` at worker_startup.template.sh lines 28-30, while the revised plan says to assert it is exported consistently when proving both wgp and vibecomfy render from the same template. if implemented as an unconditional wgp+vibecomfy export assertion, the test would conflict with the current template's intentional conditional behavior.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-12-dual-executor-20260507-0226"
        ]
      }
    ]

  {
    "subsystem": "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure",
    "total_occurrences": 6,

    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-014",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-110",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },

        "id": "DEBT-142",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",

        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: documentation/data gap: the sprint exit criteria require profiles 1 and 3 to have vram and wall-clock data, but the plan puts that only in metadata as an info-level criterion and does not schedule a concrete artifact location or command for recording the measurements. the implementation could satisfy all unit tests while still missing one of the explicit exit criteria.",

        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting infrastructure for resolution is still incomplete if direct vibecomfy routes are considered production-supported. vibecomfy has a `vibecomfy.patches.resolution` helper and the cli can load scratchpad files with `allow_scratchpad=true`, but the plan does not require using that path; it permits leaving resolution out for sprint 2.",

      {
        "id": "DEBT-109",
        "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",

        "plan_ids": [

- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

        Additional guidelines:
        - Robustness level: standard. Use balanced judgment. Flag significant risks, but do not spend flags on minor polish or executor-obvious boilerplate.
        - Over-engineering: prefer the simplest approach that fully solves the problem.
        - Reuse existing flag IDs when the same concern is still open.
        - `verified_flag_ids`: list flag IDs from prior iterations that the revised plan actually resolves (e.g., if the plan was revised to fix FLAG-001, and you confirm the fix is correct, include "FLAG-001"). Only include flags you've verified — don't guess.
        - Verify that the plan follows the expected structure when validator warnings or the outline suggest drift.
        - Additional flags may use these categories: correctness, security, completeness, performance, maintainability, other.
        - Focus on concrete issues, not structural formatting.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: VibeComfy/Wan2GP Parity Closure With Defensible LTX Promotion

## Overview
The critique shows the previous plan was targeting the right subsystem, but the first promotion slice was too broad. The root issue is still route/contract drift between VibeComfy templates and worker route contracts, but not every route that points at an LTX first/last template is safe to promote. The available `video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame` ready template hardcodes distilled LTX assets, so promoting the non-distilled `ltx2` route would violate the user's exact Wan2GP parity bar.

Revised approach: promote only routes whose model variant, assets, doctor checks, adapter bindings, contracts, matrix metadata, and docs are defensible. The `ltx2_distilled` no-control first/last route can be considered for promotion only after VibeComfy doctor and model-asset declaration gaps are fixed. The non-distilled `ltx2` route must remain fail-closed unless execution adds a real non-distilled template or model-switching path with tests.

Existing tooling remains the foundation: VibeComfy owns workflow conversion, pure-Python ready templates, template index, `validate`, `doctor`, and model/custom-node asset checks. `reigh-worker` owns route selection, capability contracts, app inventory, matrix manifests, live evidence links, and tracker rendering. `reigh-worker-orchestrator` and `runpod-lifecycle` are validation scope only unless route metadata or pod lifecycle behavior changes.

## Phase 1: Reconcile Existing Drift Before Promotion

### Step 1: Re-audit the current LTX route state (`reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py`, `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/matrix.py`, `reigh-worker/scripts/capability_contracts/live_matrix_manifest.json`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Confirm which LTX route keys are currently marked `vibecomfy_supported`, `vibecomfy_unsupported`, or WGP-only.
2. Confirm current matrix case names from `build_matrix()` and compare them to `live_matrix_manifest.json` before relying on capability-contract tests.
3. Treat matrix-manifest drift as an immediate blocking consistency issue, not a later documentation cleanup.

### Step 2: Fail closed for non-defensible model variants (`reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py`, `reigh-worker/scripts/capability_contracts/contracts.json`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Keep or return `travel_segment__model-ltx2__guidance-none__continuity-first_last__profile-default` to `vibecomfy_unsupported` unless a non-distilled LTX template or adapter-level model switch is implemented in the same slice.
2. Record the blocker explicitly: the available ready template uses distilled LTX assets and does not preserve the non-distilled `ltx2_22B` model variant.
3. Keep all LTX control-guide routes fail-closed until there is separate control-capable template evidence.

### Step 3: Fix matrix manifest drift early (`reigh-worker/scripts/capability_contracts/live_matrix_manifest.json`, `reigh-worker/tests/test_capability_contracts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Add or correct manifest entries for any current `build_matrix()` cases that already exist but are missing from `live_matrix_manifest.json`.
2. Do not add live evidence to contracts just because a case exists in the manifest.
3. Add or keep a regression gate that ensures `python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report validate --json` and `tests/test_capability_contracts.py` expose route/manifest drift.

## Phase 2: Make the Distilled LTX Template Promotable

### Step 4: Make VibeComfy doctor a hard static gate (`vibecomfy/ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` if needed)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Run `python -m vibecomfy.cli doctor video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame --json` and inspect the GetNode/SetNode broadcast runtime-link errors.
2. Fix the ready template or doctor logic only where the graph is genuinely invalid or the diagnostic is incorrectly treating valid ready-template policy behavior as an error.
3. Do not promote any route using this template while doctor exits nonzero.
4. Preserve pure-Python ready-template execution; do not fall back to JSON workflow execution.

### Step 5: Declare LTX model assets explicitly (`vibecomfy/ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame.py`, `vibecomfy/tests/test_model_assets.py`, `vibecomfy/tests/test_ready_templates.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Add model asset declarations for every concrete LTX model file referenced by the template: UNET/GGUF, CLIP/projection, video/audio/tiny VAE, spatial upscaler, and any distilled LoRA actually used.
2. Keep `READY_REQUIREMENTS["custom_nodes"]` aligned with the template's node classes.
3. Add a focused test that fails if this ready template references model filenames absent from `READY_METADATA["model_assets"]` or `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]`.
4. Run model-asset tests before updating worker contracts.

### Step 6: Prove worker adapter bindings, not only template metadata (`reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/vibecomfy_adapter.py`, `reigh-worker/tests/test_vibecomfy_backend_selection.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Inspect the LTX scratchpad writer in `vibecomfy_adapter.py` and verify the actual production binding path for start/end images, width, height, duration, FPS, prompt, negative prompt, and seed.
2. Add or update tests that assert the generated/patched scratchpad for the distilled LTX route preserves those Wan2GP settings.
3. Do not rely on `READY_METADATA["unbound_inputs"]` alone as proof of runtime parity, because the worker adapter is the binding surface for this path.

## Phase 3: Promote Only the Defensible Route

### Step 7: Promote the distilled no-control route contract only after static gates are green (`reigh-worker/scripts/capability_contracts/contracts.json`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Promote `travel_segment__model-ltx2_distilled__guidance-none__continuity-first_last__profile-default` to `implementation: vibecomfy` only after doctor, model-asset, template-index, and adapter-binding tests pass.
2. Set `template_id` to `video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame`.
3. Use `status: static_valid` or `template_bound`, not `runtime_green`, until completed live evidence exists.
4. Update artifact contract fields for child task completion, MP4 output, and storage/output expectations.

### Step 8: Keep the non-distilled route explicitly blocked (`reigh-worker/scripts/capability_contracts/contracts.json`, `reigh-worker/docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Ensure `travel_segment__model-ltx2__guidance-none__continuity-first_last__profile-default` remains unsupported/fail-closed unless a non-distilled implementation was added.
2. The blocker must name the exact missing requirement: non-distilled model parity is not preserved by the current distilled ready template.
3. Confirm docs make the distinction between `ltx2` and `ltx2_distilled` clear.

### Step 9: Regenerate tracker and verify route-contract coverage (`reigh-worker/docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Run `python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report render docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md` after contract edits.
2. Confirm the distilled no-control route appears as VibeComfy static/template-bound, the non-distilled route remains fail-closed, and all LTX control routes remain fail-closed.

## Phase 4: Optional Runtime Evidence

### Step 10: Add or use current live matrix cases without claiming proof prematurely (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/matrix.py`, `reigh-worker/scripts/capability_contracts/live_matrix_manifest.json`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Use current matrix case names only; do not resurrect `travel_segment_ltx2_control_video_first_last`.
2. Add stable manifest entries for LTX distilled no-control cases if missing.
3. Keep live evidence empty until an actual completed report with output artifact exists.

### Step 11: Run live evidence only when non-duplicative (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Launch targeted RunPod/live matrix work only if static gates are green and it will not duplicate main-thread live-test work.
2. If launched, record pod IDs, report paths, final statuses, output artifact locations, and termination evidence.
3. Promote to `runtime_green` only for routes with completed reports and output artifacts.

## Execution Order
1. Reconcile current route and matrix-manifest drift first so the capability-contract suite is meaningful.
2. Fail closed for non-distilled `ltx2` unless a true non-distilled implementation is added.
3. Fix VibeComfy doctor and model-asset declarations before any contract promotion.
4. Prove worker adapter bindings for distilled LTX first/last settings.
5. Promote only the distilled no-control route to VibeComfy static/template-bound status.
6. Regenerate docs and run static/test gates.
7. Add runtime evidence only after static gates pass and live execution is intentionally scheduled.

## Validation Order
1. `cd reigh-worker && python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report validate --json`
2. `cd reigh-worker && python -m pytest tests/test_capability_contracts.py -q`
3. `cd vibecomfy && python -m tools.refresh_template_index --check`
4. `cd vibecomfy && python -m vibecomfy.cli validate video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame`
5. `cd vibecomfy && python -m vibecomfy.cli doctor video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame --json`
6. `cd vibecomfy && python -m pytest tests/test_ready_templates.py tests/test_model_assets.py -q`
7. `cd reigh-worker && python -m pytest tests/test_template_routing.py tests/test_vibecomfy_backend_selection.py -q`
8. `cd reigh-worker-orchestrator && python -m pytest tests/gpu_orchestrator/test_route_aware_task_counts.py tests/test_route_stale_worker_state.py -q` only if route metadata shape changes.
9. Optional runtime: targeted current LTX live matrix case after all static gates are green.

## Remaining Blockers
- The non-distilled `ltx2` route cannot be promoted to the current distilled template without violating model parity.
- `vibecomfy doctor` must pass for `video/ltx2_3_runexx_first_last_frame` before any route using it is promoted.
- The LTX first/last template needs explicit model-asset declarations for the concrete model files it references.
- Current matrix-manifest drift must be fixed before `tests/test_capability_contracts.py` can be trusted as a green gate.
- LTX control routes remain unsupported until a control-capable VibeComfy path is proven.
- Runtime evidence is still required before any LTX route can be marked `runtime_green`.

  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-143",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"

    {
      "id": "DEBT-156",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures.",

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: route support states: the revised step 5 says to classify ltx control rows as vibecomfy_supported, wgp_only, vibecomfy_unsupported, new, or blocked, but the current worker routesupportstate enum at reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py:17-20 only supports wgp_only, vibecomfy_supported, and vibecomfy_unsupported. the plan needs to either extend the enum/report schema for new/blocked or keep new/blocked strictly as fixture disposition values mapped to an existing runtime support state.",

  ],
  "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-108",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-145",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-153",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: worker integration: the migration doc describes the per-call profile override gap as involving reigh-worker adapter handling of override_profile before constructing vibecomfy sessionconfig, but the plan limits sprint 1 per-run work to the vibecomfy cli/runtime. that may be acceptable for a vibecomfy-library mvp, but it does not fully exercise the production-facing per-task override semantics called out in the overall context.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-155",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: a residual issue-hint tension remains for resolution handling. the plan acknowledges current `vibecomfy run` lacks a resolution flag, but allows the adapter to leave resolution and other template-specific values unwired for sprint 2. because `z_image_turbo` and `qwen_image_2512` are marked vibecomfy-supported direct routes, this may not preserve the existing worker task-parameter contract for tasks that provide a non-default `resolution`.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-142",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-154",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: documentation/data gap: the sprint exit criteria require profiles 1 and 3 to have vram and wall-clock data, but the plan puts that only in metadata as an info-level criterion and does not schedule a concrete artifact location or command for recording the measurements. the implementation could satisfy all unit tests while still missing one of the explicit exit criteria.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting infrastructure for resolution is still incomplete if direct vibecomfy routes are considered production-supported. vibecomfy has a `vibecomfy.patches.resolution` helper and the cli can load scratchpad files with `allow_scratchpad=true`, but the plan does not require using that path; it permits leaving resolution out for sprint 2.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-162",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: checked vibecomfy profile wording against `vibecomfy/tests/test_ready_templates.py`. the revised plan says to add the production template to representative compile profile coverage 'alongside the existing dry-run id', but the dry-run id is not currently in `profile_smoke_template_ids`; only `video/wanvideo_wrapper_22_5b_i2v` and `video/wan_t2v` are. this is a small wording mismatch rather than a functional blocker because adding either the production id alone or both ids would still satisfy sprint 4.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-146",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: finding callers of the changed module shows that `shared.utils.self_refiner` is consumed by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx2_handler.py`, multiple ltx2 pipeline modules, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`. the revised validation steps still only drive the three bridge getters in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`; none of the scheduled commands execute a real `self_refiner` caller, so the plan does not yet verify the main caller shapes for the other explicit sprint 2 file change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-159",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked direct queue caller values in `db_task_to_generation_task()`: direct image tasks may carry `resolution`, `steps`/`num_inference_steps`, `seed`, and `override_profile`. the revised adapter plan covers seed, steps, and memory profile but not guaranteed resolution propagation, so it does not handle all relevant direct-route caller arguments for a supported vibecomfy direct route.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    }
  ],

        Escalated debt subsystems:
        [
  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 9,
    "plan_count": 6,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-143",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-156",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-164",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: route support states: the revised step 5 says to classify ltx control rows as vibecomfy_supported, wgp_only, vibecomfy_unsupported, new, or blocked, but the current worker routesupportstate enum at reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py:17-20 only supports wgp_only, vibecomfy_supported, and vibecomfy_unsupported. the plan needs to either extend the enum/report schema for new/blocked or keep new/blocked strictly as fixture disposition values mapped to an existing runtime support state.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-9-control-rail-and-20260506-1802"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-165",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: startup tests: step 11 still needs sharper wording for `vibecomfy_memory_profile`. the current startup template exports it only inside `if [ \"$reigh_backend\" = \"vibecomfy\" ] && [[ \"$reigh_worker_profile\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]` at worker_startup.template.sh lines 28-30, while the revised plan says to assert it is exported consistently when proving both wgp and vibecomfy render from the same template. if implemented as an unconditional wgp+vibecomfy export assertion, the test would conflict with the current template's intentional conditional behavior.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-12-dual-executor-20260507-0226"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },

  {
    "subsystem": "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure",
    "total_occurrences": 6,
    "plan_count": 6,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-014",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-110",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-142",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-154",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: documentation/data gap: the sprint exit criteria require profiles 1 and 3 to have vram and wall-clock data, but the plan puts that only in metadata as an info-level criterion and does not schedule a concrete artifact location or command for recording the measurements. the implementation could satisfy all unit tests while still missing one of the explicit exit criteria.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [

        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-158",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting infrastructure for resolution is still incomplete if direct vibecomfy routes are considered production-supported. vibecomfy has a `vibecomfy.patches.resolution` helper and the cli can load scratchpad files with `allow_scratchpad=true`, but the plan does not require using that path; it permits leaving resolution out for sprint 2.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

        "id": "DEBT-162",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: checked vibecomfy profile wording against `vibecomfy/tests/test_ready_templates.py`. the revised plan says to add the production template to representative compile profile coverage 'alongside the existing dry-run id', but the dry-run id is not currently in `profile_smoke_template_ids`; only `video/wanvideo_wrapper_22_5b_i2v` and `video/wan_t2v` are. this is a small wording mismatch rather than a functional blocker because adding either the production id alone or both ids would still satisfy sprint 4.",

  {
    "subsystem": "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 5,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-013",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-108",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-145",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",

        "plan_ids": [

        "id": "DEBT-153",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: worker integration: the migration doc describes the per-call profile override gap as involving reigh-worker adapter handling of override_profile before constructing vibecomfy sessionconfig, but the plan limits sprint 1 per-run work to the vibecomfy cli/runtime. that may be acceptable for a vibecomfy-library mvp, but it does not fully exercise the production-facing per-task override semantics called out in the overall context.",

        "id": "DEBT-109",
        "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",

- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
